TRANSPORT DEPARTMENT

NOTIFICATION

Shimla-02, the 20th january, 2026

No. TPT-F(4)-3/2024-III.—WHEREAS, the draft State Transport Undertaking Scheme,
2025 was notified vide this department notification No. TPT-F(4)-3/2024 dated 21st January, 2025
and published in the Rajpatra (e-Gazette), Himachal Pradesh on dated 22nd January, 2025 for
inviting objection(s) and suggestion(s) from the person(s) likely to be affected thereby, as required
under the provisions of Section 99 of the Motor Vehicles Act, 1988 (No. 59 of 1988);

AND WHEREAS, total 1148 objection(s) and suggestion(s) from 104 objectors were
received by the State Government within prescribed 30 days period, while the remaining 34
objection from 03 objectors were received after the exipry of 30 days, the objection(s) and
suggestions(s) were duly heard by the Government on 15-07-2025 and were decided and is
included in the final notification;

NOW, THEREFORE, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 107 read with section
99 of the Motor Vehicles Act, 1988 (No. 59 of 1988) and rule 124 of the Himachal Pradesh Motor
Vehicles Rules, 1999, the Governor, Himachal Pradesh is pleased to notify the Himachal Pradesh
State Transport Undertaking Scheme, 2025 as under:—

STATE TRANSPORT UNDERTAKING SCHEME-2025

1. Short title.—This Scheme may be called the Himachal Pradesh Transport Services of
the State Transport Undertaking Scheme, 2025,

2. Definitions.—(1) In this Scheme, unless the context otherwise requires;

(a) “Act” means the Motor Vehicles Act, 1988 (Act No.59 of 1988);

(b) “Fleet Strength” means the buses owned and operated by the State Transport
Undertaking including buses of Himachal Pradesh City Transport and Bus Stands
Management and Development Authority being operated under MoU at any point
of time;

(c) “Government” means the Government of Himachal Pradesh;

(d) “Schedule” means the Schedule appended to this Scheme; and

(e) “State Transport Undertaking” means Himachal Road Transport Corporation.

(2) All other words and expressions’ used herein but not defined shall have the same
meaning as assigned to them in the Motor Vehicle Act, 1988.

HP FORM XLVI S.S.TU
(Rule 124 of the Motor Vehicles Rules, 1999)

1. Full name of the Undertaking.—Himachal Road Transport Corporation

2. Address.—Head Quarter at Old Busstand Shimla 171003

3. Route/Routes of area/Areas covered by the scheme:—

As per Schedule of 2484 routes appended with the scheme.

3.1 On the inter District routes private sector shall continue to operate with State Transport
Undertaking on the basis of permits granted under Chapter-V of the Act as hereto
force.

4. Whether the undertaking is proposed to operate the road transport service on the
said route/s area covered by the scheme to the exclusion, complete or partial or other persons
or otherwise.

4.1 Private sector participation shall continue on the existing route permits granted under
Chapter V of the Motor Vehicles Act to ply as stage carriages. However, where
adequate service is not provided by the private sector, the Himachal Road Transport
Corporation shall provide the service.

5. The names and addresses of existing permit holders operating on the route/s or on
the area mentioned in (3) above:—

5.1 The routes permits already issued to private operators and other State Transport
Undertakings.

6. The nature of the services proposed to be rendered:—

6.1 The state Transport Undertaking will ply services as Stage Carriage.

7. The maximum number of vehicles proposed to ply at any one time under the
scheme:

7.1 As per fleet of the State Transport Undertaking.

8. The minimum number of vehicles proposed to ply at any one time under the
scheme and the minimum number of daily trips:

8.1 As per fleet of the State Transport Undertaking.

9. Type or Types of vehicles proposed to be uses on the service and approximate
seating capacity:

9.1 Vehicles of not less than 14 and not more than 52 seats.

10. Number of trips proposed to be run to be charged:—As per Schedule-1 attached.

11. Standard rate of fare proposed to be charged:—As per the Notification issued by
State Govt. time to time.

12. The date from which the undertaking is proposed to commence the operation of
the service (if dates are different for various routes or areas, mention the same):

12.1 From the date of Publication of Draft STU Scheme onwords.

13. The name of Regional Transport Authorities under whose jurisdictions the
route(s) or area or any portion thereof:- As per the schedule -2

14. Any other information:—

14.1 Grant of Permits to State Transport Undertaking and Private Sector:—

(1) Permits to the State Transport Undertakings shall be granted by the State
Transport Authority or Regional Transport Authority, as the case may be, under
Section 103 of the Motor Vehicles Act on fleet strength, on the prescribed
application forms specified under the Himachal Pradesh Motor Vehicles Rules,
1999.

(2) Permits for private sector shall continue to be regulated under Chapter–V of the
Act.

14.2 Conditions of Operation of Services:—

The Himachal Pradesh State Road Transport Corporation when granted a permit to
operate Road Transport Services on the routes or portion thereof as mentioned in the
Schedule shall abide by the following conditions:—

(a) it shall ensure uninterrupted services to the general public;

(b) it shall provide free or concessional transport facility to such categories of
persons as may be specified by the State Government;

(c) it shall maintain fleet strength to provide uninterrupted services to the general
public on the routes or portion thereof as mentioned in the Schedule;

(d) it shall operate services on the basis of time table fixed by the Transport
Authority; and

(e) it shall deploy buses from its fleet strength to maintain or restore service in case
of breakdown of service on any particular route or its portion thereof.

(f) The routes published under the STU/scheme shall be in relaxation to the 60:40
norm of the Transport Policy, 2014.

14.3 Operation of additional services by the State Transport Undertaking:—

The Himachal Pradesh State Road Transport Corporation may, in the public interest,
operate additional services on payment for the conveyance of passengers on special occasions such
as to and from fairs and religious gatherings;

Provided that the Himachal Pradesh State Road Transport Corporation shall inform the
concerned Transport Authority regarding the operation of such additional services without delay.

14.4 Scheme to be laid before the State Legislative/Assembly:—

The Scheme shall be laid before the State Legislative Assembly as soon as may be after it is
made.

14. 5 Repeal and Savings:—

(1) The Himachal Pradesh Road Transport Services of State Transport Undertaking
2006 is hereby repealed.

(2) Notwithstanding such repeal any action taken or anything done under the scheme
so repealed shall be deemed to have been done or taken under the corresponding
provisions of this scheme.

By order,

R.D. NAZEEM, IAS,
Additional Chief Secretary (Transport).
